# Production completion checklist — 20 November 2026

This is the execution ledger for the supplied complete-production directive. Existing local workflows are not automatically marked VERIFIED. Statuses: NOT_STARTED, IN_PROGRESS, IMPLEMENTED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED. SPEC rows retain every bullet/numbered requirement from the supplied brief, including acceptance gates. Priority rows track concrete implementation work; update corresponding SPEC rows when coverage is established. Dependencies requiring external services or policy decisions must name the exact dependency; other unfinished work remains NOT_STARTED, not BLOCKED.

## Scope and next incomplete work

The full directive is preserved in PRODUCTION_DIRECTIVE.md. This delivery is an ongoing implementation, not a declaration of production readiness. SPEC rows are conservative until individually traced; baseline functionality and limitations are described in ARCHITECTURE.md and KNOWN_LIMITATIONS.md. NOT_STARTED means unfinished work, not an external blocker.

Immediate next work: complete configurable company administration and effective-dated schedule assignments (including historical absences without sessions), then employee profile/email-change verification, platform administrator lifecycle/roles, ownership transfer, leave policies, payroll runs/payslips/evidence, subscription entitlements, reporting/training and production operations. Nested candidates and shared form lookups now have bounded searchable pagination. Complete remaining LIST-002 configuration/filter dropdown scalability, per-list scenarios and mutable-sort consistency before effective-dated schedules.
